           │     self.sigG = False #58                                          │
           │     self.sigY = False #62                                          │
           │                                                                    │
           │   def next(self):                                                  │
           │     if (self.state == 0):                                          │
           │       self.sigR = True #74                                         │
           │       self.sigG = False #78                                        │
           │       self.sigY = False #82                                        │
           │       if (self.remaining > 0):                                     │
           │         self.remaining = (self.remaining - 1) #95                  │
           │         if self.pedestrian:                                        │
           │           self.state = 1 #102                                      │
           │           self.remaining = 60 #106                                 │
           │     else:                                                          │
           │       if (self.state == 1):                                        │
           │         self.sigR = False #121                                     │
           │         self.sigG = True #125                                      │
           │         self.sigY = False #129                                     │
           │         if self.pedestrian:                                        │
           │           if (self.remaining == 0):                                │
           │             self.state = 2 #142                                    │
           │             self.remaining = 5 #146                                │
           │           else:                                                    │
           │             self.state = 3 #151                                    │
           │             self.remaining = (60 - self.remaining) #158            │
           │       else:                                                        │
           │         if (self.state == 2):                                      │
           │           self.sigR = False #172                                   │
           │           self.sigG = False #176                                   │
           │           self.sigY = True #180                                    │
           │           if (self.remaining > 0):                                 │
           │             self.remaining = (self.remaining - 1) #193             │
           │           else:                                                    │
           │             self.state = 0 #198                                    │
           │             self.remaining = 60 #202                               │
           │         else:                                                      │
           │           if (self.state == 3):                                    │
           │             self.sigR = False #214                                 │
           │             self.sigG = False #218                                 │
           │             self.sigY = False #222                                 │
           │             if (self.remaining > 0):                               │
           │               self.remaining = (self.remaining - 1) #235           │
           │             else:                                                  │
           │               self.state = 2 #240                                  │
           │               self.remaining = 5 #244                              │
           │                                                                    │
           │   def specification(self):                                         │
           │     self.count_inv = ((((self.remaining >= 0) and (self.remaining  │
           │ <= 60)) and (self.state == 2)) and (self.remaining <= 5))          │
           │     self.state_inv = ((self.state >= 0) and (self.state <= 3))     │
           │     self.remaining_inv = (((self.remaining >= 0) and               │
           │ (self.remaining <= 60)) if (self.state == 3) else True)            │
           │     self.sigr_inv = (self.sigR == (self.state == 0))               │
           │     self.sigg_inv = (self.sigG == (self.state == 1))               │
